Implementing LSE Adam details:

Represent p as softmax(z) with sum1. At each iteration:
```
p = softmax_target(z,1)
b = conv_fft(p, conv_size)
M = max(b)
score = 2*n*M
# track best p, score
if low beta:
   w = softmax(beta*b)
else use active weight.
h = gradient(p, w, grad_size) # 2*...
# z-gradient:
mu = np.dot(h,p)
gz = p*(h-mu)
# normalize gz for step?
gnorm = norm(gz)
if gnorm>0: gz/=gnorm
# Adam update on gz
m*(etc)
z = z - lr*gm
z = center_clip_z(z)
# optional small noise
```

A direct and powerful method: Use projected gradient in probability space:
- At p, compute w weights (LSE or active), h=gradient.
- Descent direction can be p - u, where u is a smoothing of gradient low? For linear objective over simplex, minimizer u=categorical min. def _next_p2(m)

def _local_score_full(a): b=conv; return 2*n*max(b)/S²
def _make_box_target(b, S2, level): ...
def _spectral_sqrt_step(a, t, L): ...
def _flatten_search(a, max_seconds, start_level, min_level, step_levels, rng):
    a=normalize(a)
    best=a copy; best_score=local_score
    level = min(start_level, best_score? maybe if start_level >=best_score, reduce)
    # generate levels descending from best_score * 0.995 to min_level

          if score2 < best_score: update best, a=best? For next level use best a (not a2 if worse). Maybe also continue from a2 if it passes level? Use best.
       level -= 0.01 or level *=0.995
    return best,best_score
```

def _flatten_search(a_init, max_seconds, min_level, initial_level_delta, rng):
    n=len(a_init); S=target sqrt(2n)
    a=normalize(a_init)
    best=a copy; best_score=local_score(a)
    L=_fft_len(n)
    level = best_score - initial_level_delta
    if level > min_level:
       while time < deadline and level > min_level:
          # maybe if current best_score <= level: level -=
          base_a=best.copy(); base_score=best_score
          for damp in (0.1,0.2,0.35,0.6,1.0):
             a2=base_a.copy(); s2=base_score; fail=0
             for it in range(80):
                b=conv(a2); M=max(b)
                if M <= level*(1+1e-7): break
                t=_make_target(b,S2,level)
                if t is None: break
                anew=_spectral_sqrt(a2,t,L)
                # normalize anew
                cand=(1-damp)*a2 + damp*anew
                cand=clip; normalize; sc=score
                if sc < s2 - 1e-12:
                   a2=cand; s2=sc; fail=0
                   if sc < best_score:
                      best=a2.copy(); best_score=sc; print
                      if sc <= level: break
                else:
                   fail +=1
                   if fail>6: break
             if s2 < best_score: best=a2.copy(); best_score=s2
          level -= 0.01
       # maybe if best > min, try level min
    return best,best_score
```

Proportional target code:
```
def _make_box_target(b, S2, level):
    L=len(b)
    avg=S2/L
    if not np.isfinite(level) or level <= avg*(1+1e-9): return None
    t=np.asarray(b,dtype=np.float64).copy()
    t[t<0]=0
    high=t>level
    t[high]=level
    missing=S2 - t.sum(dtype=np.float64)
    if missing > 0:
        room=level - t
        total_room=room.sum(dtype=np.float64)
        if total_room <= missing* (1+1e-12):
            # still not enough due to numerical; distribute somewhat
            if total_room <=0:
                return None
            alpha = missing / total_room
            t += alpha*room
    elif missing < 0: # rare
        scale=S2 / max(t.sum(),1e-300)
        t *= scale
        t=np.minimum(t, level)
        # if sum low, scale up all? but maxim exceed; ignore
    t=np.clip(t,0,level)
    # final sum adjust:
    s=t.sum()
    if s>0 and abs(s-S2)>1e-8*S2:
       t *= S2/s
       # if exceeds level due to scale, redistribute quickly:
       over=t>level
       if np.any(over):
           excess=(t-overlevel).*? ...
```
Maybe avoid final scale; after adding proportional, sum should S2 within floating. Clip could reduce sum a hair; ignore. We can calculate and if reduction small, add to first free? Not important because target sum for spectral not critical? It should be S2 to match norm. But small error okay. We can enforce by `t *= S2 / t.sum()` then if `t.max()>level+1e-9`, reduce top by trimming and adding? Simpler:
```
s=t.sum(); t *= S2/s
t=np.minimum(t, level)
s=t.sum()
if s < S2:
   diff=S2-s
   room=level-t
   if room.sum()>0: t += diff/room.sum()*room
```
Good.

Spectral step:
```
def _spectral_sqrt_step(a, t, L):
    A=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    B=np.fft.rfft(t,L)
    maxA=np.max(np.abs(A)) if A.size else 1
    tol=1e-10*maxA
    A_safe=A.copy()
    small=np.abs(A_safe)<tol
    if np.any(small): A_safe[small]=tol
    update=np.fft.irfft(0.5*(A + B/A_safe), L).real
    return update[:len(a)]
```
Need complex A: rfft returns complex; `A_safe[small]=tol` sets real positive. Good.

```
def _add_candidate(cands, arr, targetS):
    a=np.asarray(arr,dtype=float)
    if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0: return
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return
    s=float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if s<=1e-12: return
    a=a*(targetS/s)
    sc=_local_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc): cands.append((a,sc))
```

```python
"""
Hybrid search for nonnegative step heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

The main new ingredient over the previous approach is a spectral
"flatten-the-square" projection:
  1. from the current sequence compute its self-convolution b;
  2. build a target b' with a lower maximum by capping high coefficients
     and redistributing mass into valleys while preserving sum(b') = sum(b);
  3. take a damped Newton square-root step in the circular FFT domain,
     clip negatives, blend with the old sequence;
  4. descend the target maximum until time is exhausted.

The best sequence found by this projection is then polished by a
mirror-descent / log-sum-exp local search in log-space.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _next_p2(m):
    """Smallest power of two >= m."""
    m = int(m)
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _fft_len(n):
    return _next_p2(max(1, 2 * int(n) - 1))


def _sqrt_series(n):
    """
    Coefficients of (1-x)^(-1/2), a strong fallback that approaches pi/2.
    """
    n = int(n)
    a = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 0:
        return a
    a[0] = 1.0
    for k in range(1, n):
        a[k] = a[k - 1] * float(2 * k - 1) / float(2 * k)
    return a


def _normalize_sequence(a, target_sum):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return None
    s = float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return None
    return a * (float(target_sum) / s)


def _convolution_fft(a, L=None):
    n = int(a.size)
    if L is None:
        L = _fft_len(n)
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    C = A * A
    out = np.fft.irfft(C, L)
    return out[:max(1, 2 * n - 1)]


def _score_from_conv(a, c):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0 or c.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    M = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        return np.inf
    if M < 0.0:
        M = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * M / (S * S))


def _local_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return np.inf
    S = float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        return np.inf
    b = _convolution_fft(a)
    return _score_from_conv(a, b)


def _get_stored_sequence(max_len=16384):
    try:
        h = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        return None
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(h, dtype=np.float64)
    except Exception:
        return None
    if arr.ndim != 1 or arr.size == 0 or arr.size > max_len:
        return None
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
        return None
    return arr


def _center_clip_z(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.nan_to_num(z, nan=0.0, posinf=30.0, neginf=-30.0)
    mn = float(np.min(z))
    mx = float(np.max(z))
    rng = mx - mn
    if not np.isfinite(rng) or rng <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros_like(z)
    if rng > 60.0:
        z = z * (60.0 / rng)
    return z


def _softmax_target(z, target_sum):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    if z.size == 0:
        return z
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, -1e300)
    z = z - float(np.max(z))
    ex = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ex.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(z.shape, float(target_sum) / z.size, dtype=np.float64)
    return float(target_sum) * ex / s


def _power_weights(c, q):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    cmax = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cmax) or cmax <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    q = max(1, int(q))
    scaled = c / cmax
    w = scaled ** q
    ws = float(w.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(ws) or ws <= 0.0:
        idx = int(np.argmax(c))
        w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
        w[idx] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / ws


def _lse_weights(c, beta):
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(c.size)
    if L == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    beta = max(1e-6, float(beta))
    cm = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(cm):
        cm = 0.0
    z = beta * (c - cm)
    ex = np.exp(z)
    s = float(ex.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(L, 1.0 / L, dtype=np.float64)
    return ex / s


def _gradient(a, w, grad_size):
    """
    Gradient of sum_k w_k (a*a)[k] with respect to a.
    Returns g_j = 2 * sum_k w_k a_{k-j}.
    """
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(w.size)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([2.0 * float(a[0]) * float(w[0]) if L > 0 else 0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    rw = w[::-1]
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, grad_size)
    P = np.fft.rfft(rw, grad_size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(A * P, grad_size)
    start = L - 1
    stop = start - n
    r = conv[start:stop:-1]
    if r.size != n:
        r = conv[-n:]
    g = 2.0 * r
    return np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)


def _direction_and_scale(mode, a, b, grad_size):
    n = int(a.size)
    L = int(b.size)
    if mode == -1:
        # L2 flattening: push length-2 profile toward its mean.
        y = float(b.sum(dtype=np.float64)) / float(max(1, L))
        w = b - y
        g = _gradient(a, w, grad_size)
        scale = float(np.max(np.abs(g))) if g.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
            return None, 0.0
        return g / scale, 1.0
    elif mode < -1:
        beta = float(-mode)
        w = _lse_weights(b, beta)
        g = _gradient(a, w, grad_size)
        scale = float(np.max(g)) if g.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
            return None, 0.0
        return np.maximum(g, 0.0) / scale, 1.0
    else:
        w = _power_weights(b, mode)
        g = _gradient(a, w, grad_size)
        scale = float(np.max(g)) if g.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 1e-300:
            return None, 0.0
        return np.maximum(g, 0.0) / scale, 1.0


def _optimize_mode(a_init, max_seconds, mode, rng, label, hard_deadline):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a_init, math.sqrt(2.0 * int(a_init.size)))
    if a is None:
        n = 8192
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    n = int(a.size)
    targetS = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    a = _normalize_sequence(a, targetS)
    if a is None:
        a = np.full(n, targetS / n, dtype=np.float64)

    z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))
    z = _center_clip_z(z)
    a = _softmax_target(z, targetS)

    conv_size = _fft_len(n)
    grad_size = _next_p2(max(1, 3 * n - 2))
    b = _convolution_fft(a, conv_size)
    score = _score_from_conv(a, b)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    best_z = z.copy()

    if max_seconds <= 0.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = min(start + float(max_seconds), float(hard_deadline))
    if deadline - start <= 0.0:
        return best_a, best_score

    if mode == -1:
        step = 0.18
    elif mode < -1:
        step = 0.30
    else:
        step = 0.40

    fail = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = 0.0

    try:
        print(f"[local:{label}] mode={mode} n={n} start={score:.9f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        d, _ = _direction_and_scale(mode, a, b, grad_size)
        if d is None:
            break

        if fail > 45:
            noise = 0.03 + 0.03 * min(1.0, float(fail) / 100.0)
            z = best_z.copy() + noise * rng.standard_normal(n)
            z = _center_clip_z(z)
            a = _softmax_target(z, targetS)
            b = _convolution_fft(a, conv_size)
            score = _score_from_conv(a, b)
            step = 0.20
            fail = 0
            continue

        if mode == -1:
            factors = (0.25, 0.50, 1.00, 1.50)
        else:
            factors = (0.25, 0.50, 1.00, 2.00)

        best_cand_score = score
        best_cand = None

        for fac in factors:
            st = float(step) * float(fac)
            if st <= 0.0:
                continue
            zt = z - st * d
            if fail > 10 and fac == 1.0 and rng.random() < 0.18:
                zt = zt + 0.02 * rng.standard_normal(n)
            zt = _center_clip_z(zt)
            at = _softmax_target(zt, targetS)
            bt = _convolution_fft(at, conv_size)
            sc = _score_from_conv(at, bt)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_cand_score:
                best_cand_score = sc
                best_cand = (zt, at, bt, sc, st)

        if best_cand is not None and best_cand_score < score - 1e-12:
            z, a, b, score, chosen_step = best_cand
            step = min(2.0, max(0.02, float(chosen_step) * 1.08))
            fail = 0
            if score < best_score:
                best_score = score
                best_a = a.copy()
                best_z = z.copy()
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now - last_print > 5.0:
                    try:
                        print(f"[local:{label}] mode={mode} best={score:.9f} it={it}")
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                    last_print = now
        else:
            step *= 0.70
            fail += 1
            if step < 0.012:
                step = 0.18 if mode == -1 else 0.35

    return best_a, best_score


def _local_search(a_init, max_seconds, rng, label, hard_deadline):
    a = _normalize_sequence(a_init, math.sqrt(2.0 * int(a_init.size)))
    if a is None:
        n = 8192
        a = np.full(n, np.sqrt(2.0 * n) / n, dtype=np.float64)
    best_score = _local_score(a)
    best_a = a.copy()

    schedule = [
        (-1, 0.10),
        (-2, 0.15),
        (-8, 0.20),
        (-32, 0.30),
        (32, 0.25),
    ]

    for mode, frac in schedule:
        t = float(max_seconds) * float(frac)
        t = min(t, max(0.0, float(hard_deadline) - time.monotonic()))
        if t <= 1.5:
            continue
        seq2, score2 = _optimize_mode(best_a, t, int(mode), rng, label, hard_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(score2) and score2 < best_score:
            best_score = score2
            best_a = np.asarray(seq2, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    return best_a, best_score


def _make_box_target(b, S2, level):
    """
    For b = a*a, build t >= 0, max(t) <= level, sum(t) = S2,
    approximately close to b with high coefficients capped and valleys raised.
    """
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64)
    L = int(b.size)
    if L == 0:
        return None
    level = float(level)
    S2 = float(S2)
    if not np.isfinite(level) or level <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(S2) or S2 <= 0.0:
        return None
    avg = S2 / float(L)
    if level <= avg * (1.0 + 1e-9):
        return None

    t = b.copy()
    t = np.clip(t, 0.0, level)

    s = float(t.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    missing = S2 - s
    if missing > 1e-10:
        room = level - t
        total_room = float(room.sum(dtype=np.float64))
        if total_room <= 1e-12 or total_room < missing * (1.0 - 1e-12):
            return None
        t += (missing / total_room) * room

    elif missing < -1e-10:
        s0 = float(t.sum(dtype=np.float64))
        if s0 <= 1e-12:
            return None
        t *= (S2 / s0)
        over = t > level
        if np.any(over):
            excess = float((t[over] - level).sum(dtype=np.float64))
            t[over] = level
            if excess > 0.0:
                room2 = level - t
                total_room2 = float(room2.sum(dtype=np.float64))
                if total_room2 > 0.0:
                    t += (excess / total_room2) * room2

    t = np.clip(t, 0.0, level)
    s = float(t.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if s > 0.0 and abs(s - S2) > 1e-9 * S2:
        t *= (S2 / s)
        over = t > level
        if np.any(over):
            excess = float((t[over] - level).sum(dtype=np.float64))
            t[over] = level
            if excess > 1e-12:
                room = level - t
                total_room = float(room.sum(dtype=np.float64))
                if total_room > 0.0 and excess <= total_room * (1.0 + 1e-10):
                    t += (excess / total_room) * room

    return t


def _spectral_sqrt_step(a, target, L):
    """
    One damped polynomial-square-root / spectral Newton step:
        A_new ~ (A + target / A) / 2   in the circular FFT ring.
    """
    A = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    B = np.fft.rfft(target, L)
    absA = np.abs(A)
    scale = float(np.max(absA)) if absA.size else 1.0
    if not np.isfinite(scale) or scale <= 0.0:
        scale = 1.0
    tol = 1e-9 * scale
    A_safe = A.copy()
    small = np.abs(A_safe) < tol
    if np.any(small):
        A_safe[small] = tol + 0.0j
    upd = 0.5 * (A + B / A_safe)
    out = np.fft.irfft(upd, L).real
    return out[:a.size]


def _flatten_search(a_init, max_seconds, min_level, rng, label, hard_deadline):
    n = int(a_init.size)
    targetS = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    S2 = float(2.0 * n)
    a = _normalize_sequence(a_init, targetS)
    if a is None:
        return a_init, np.inf

    score = _local_score(a)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return a, np.inf

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = score
    L = _fft_len(n)
    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = min(start + float(max_seconds), float(hard_deadline))
    if deadline - start <= 0.5:
        return best_a, best_score

    try:
        print(f"[flatten:{label}] n={n} start={best_score:.9f} min_level={max(0.0, float(min_level)):.6f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    # Begin slightly below the current peak.
    if best_score <= 1.10:
        level = best_score - 0.003
    elif best_score <= 1.35:
        level = best_score - 0.006
    else:
        level = best_score - 0.012

    min_level = float(max(1.02, min_level))
    level = max(min_level, level)
    damps = (0.08, 0.18, 0.35, 0.60, 1.00)
    stuck_levels = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and level > min_level:
        if best_score <= level + 1e-7:
            level = max(min_level, best_score - 0.012 if best_score > 1.35 else best_score - 0.005)
        level = max(min_level, level)

        improved_this_level = False

        for damp in damps:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                break
            a2 = best_a.copy()
            s2 = best_score
            fail = 0

            for it in range(80):
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    break
                b2 = _convolution_fft(a2, L)
                m2 = float(np.max(b2))
                if not np.isfinite(m2):
                    break
                if m2 <= level * (1.0 + 1e-7):
                    break

                t = _make_box_target(b2, S2, level)
                if t is None:
                    break
                anew = _spectral_sqrt_step(a2, t, L)
                anew_clip = anew.copy()
                anew_clip[anew_clip < 0.0] = 0.0
                anew_clip = np.clip(anew_clip, 0.0, 1000.0)

                av = (1.0 - float(damp)) * a2 + float(damp) * anew_clip
                av = _normalize_sequence(av, targetS)
                if av is None:
                    fail += 1
                    if fail > 5:
                        break
                    continue

                bv = _convolution_fft(av, L)
                sc = _score_from_conv(av, bv)
                if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < s2 - 1e-12:
                    a2 = av
                    s2 = sc
                    fail = 0
                    improved_this_level = True
                    if s2 < best_score:
                        best_score = s2
                        best_a = a2.copy()
                        try:
                            print(f"[flatten:{label}] level={level:.6f} improved best={best_score:.9f}")
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                    if s2 <= level * (1.0 + 1e-7):
                        break
                else:
                    fail += 1
                    if fail > 6:
                        break

            if s2 < best_score:
                best_score = s2
                best_a = a2.copy()
                improved_this_level = True

        if improved_this_level:
            stuck_levels = 0
            level -= 0.006
        else:
            stuck_levels += 1
            if stuck_levels >= 3:
                level -= 0.012
            else:
                level -= 0.006

    return best_a, best_score


def _add_candidate(cands, arr, target_sum):
    a = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return
    s = float(a.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-12:
        return
    a = a * (float(target_sum) / s)
    sc = _local_score(a)
    if np.isfinite(sc):
        cands.append((np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy(), float(sc)))


def _candidate_key(item):
    v = float(item[1])
    if not np.isfinite(v):
        return 1e100
    return v


def _initial_sequences_for_length(n, rng, stored, topk=2):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return []
    target_sum = math.sqrt(2.0 * n)
    cands = []

    base = _sqrt_series(n)
    _add_candidate(cands, base, target_sum)
    _add_candidate(cands, base[::-1], target_sum)

    if n > 1:
        t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        for fac in (-0.20, 0.05, 0.20, 0.35):
            arr = base * (1.0 + fac * (t - 0.5))
            _add_candidate(cands, arr, target_sum)

    for alpha in (0.30, 0.45, 0.60, 0.80):
        arr = np.power(np.arange(1, n + 1, dtype=np.float64), -float(alpha))
        _add_candidate(cands, arr, target_sum)

    z = 0.25 * rng.standard_normal(n)
    z = z - float(np.mean(z))
    _add_candidate(cands, np.exp(np.clip(z, -8.0, 8.0)), target_sum)

    _add_candidate(cands, np.abs(rng.standard_normal(n)), target_sum)

    if stored is not None and int(stored.size) == n:
        _add_candidate(cands, stored, target_sum)

    if not cands:
        _add_candidate(cands, base, target_sum)

    cands.sort(key=_candidate_key)
    return [a for (a, _) in cands[:max(1, int(topk))], ]


def _candidate_candidate_score_key(item):
    return _candidate_key(item)


def _safe_exact_score(seq):
    try:
        val = evaluate_sequence(seq)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    try:
        v = float(val)
    except Exception:
        return np.inf
    if not np.isfinite(v):
        return np.inf
    return v


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=400, **kwargs):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
    now = time.monotonic()
    total_budget = max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    hard_deadline = now + total_budget

    stored = _get_stored_sequence(20000)

    # Safe fallback.
    fallback_n = 8192
    fallback = _sqrt_series(fallback_n)
    fallback = _normalize_sequence(fallback, math.sqrt(2.0 * fallback_n))
    if fallback is None:
        fallback = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    global_best = fallback.copy()
    global_score = _local_score(global_best)
    if not np.isfinite(global_score):
        global_score = 2.0

    try:
        print(f"budget={float(budget_s):.2f}s fallback_n={fallback_n} fallback_score={global_score:.9f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    if stored is not None:
        s_store = _normalize_sequence(stored, math.sqrt(2.0 * int(stored.size)))
        if s_store is not None:
            sc_store = _local_score(s_store)
            if np.isfinite(sc_store) and sc_store < global_score:
                global_score = sc_store
                global_best = s_store.copy()
                try:
                    print(f"stored length={int(stored.size)} score={global_score:.9f}")
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Decide which lengths to search.
    main_n = 8192
    if stored is not None and int(stored.size) in (4096, 8192, 16384):
        main_n = int(stored.size)

    if float(budget_s) > 260:
        jobs = [
            (main_n, 0.48),
            (8192, 0.24),
            (4096, 0.14),
            (16384, 0.14),
        ]
    elif float(budget_s) > 140:
        jobs = [
            (main_n, 0.62),
            (8192, 0.22),
            (4096, 0.16),
        ]
    else:
        jobs = [
            (main_n, 0.75),
            (8192, 0.25),
        ]

    # Deduplicate preserving fraction by adding to first occurrence.
    seen = {}
    clean_jobs = []
    for n, f in jobs:
        n = int(n)
        if n not in seen:
            seen[n] = 0.0
            clean_jobs.append((n, 0.0))
        # Add to existing slot implicitly.
        idx = next(i for i, (nn, _) in enumerate(clean_jobs) if nn == n)
        clean_jobs[idx] = (idx[0], seen[n] + float(f))
        seen[n] += float(f)
    # Normalize fractions.
    tot = sum(f for (_, f) in clean_jobs)
    if tot <= 0.0:
        clean_jobs = [(main_n, 1.0)]
    else:
        clean_jobs = [(n, f / tot) for (n, f) in clean_jobs]

    for n, frac in clean_jobs:
        t_job = float(total_budget) * float(frac)
        t_job = min(t_job, max(0.0, float(hard_deadline) - time.monotonic()))
        if t_job <= 3.0 or n <= 0:
            continue

        topk = 2 if t_job > 35.0 else 1
        cands = _initial_sequences_for_length(n, rng, stored, topk=topk)
        if not cands:
            continue

        # Split job time among candidates: first candidate gets more time.
        weights = []
        if len(cands) == 1:
            weights = [1.0]
        elif len(cands) == 2:
            weights = [0.62, 0.38]
        else:
            weights = [1.0 / len(cands)] * len(cands)

        for ci, cand in enumerate(cands):
            t_cand = t_job * weights[ci]
            t_cand = min(t_cand, max(0.0, float(hard_deadline) - time.monotonic()))
            if t_cand <= 2.0:
                break

            label = f"n{n}_c{ci}"

            t_flat = 0.42 * t_cand
            t_flat = min(t_flat, max(0.0, float(hard_deadline) - time.monotonic()))
            a2, s2 = _flatten_search(cand, t_flat, 1.30, rng, label, hard_deadline)
            if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < global_score:
                global_score = s2
                global_best = np.asarray(a2, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                try:
                    print(f"global update after flatten {label}: {global_score:.9f}")
                except Exception:
                    pass

            t_loc = 0.58 * t_cand
            t_loc = min(t_loc, max(0.0, float(hard_deadline) - time.monotonic()))
            if t_loc > 3.0:
                a2, s2 = _local_search(a2, t_loc, rng, label, hard_deadline)
                if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < global_score:
                    global_score = s2
                    global_best = np.asarray(a2, dtype=np.float64).copy()
                    try:
                        print(f"global update after local {label}: {global_score:.9f}")
                    except Exception:
                        pass

    # Final polishing on the current global best if enough time remains.
    rem = float(hard_deadline) - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 12.0:
        a3, s3 = _flatten_search(global_best, 0.18 * rem, 1.28, rng, "final_flat", hard_deadline)
        if np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < global_score:
            global_score = s3
            global_best = np.asarray(a3, dtype=np.float64).copy()

        rem = float(hard_deadline) - time.monotonic()
        if rem > 10.0:
            a3, s3 = _local_search(global_best, 0.82 * rem, rng, "final_polish", hard_deadline)
            if np.isfinite(s3) and s3 < global_score:
                global_score = s3
                global_best = np.asarray(a3, dtype=np.float64).copy()

    # Clean and normalize the returned sequence.
    out = np.asarray(global_best, dtype=np.float64)
    if out.ndim != 1 or out.size == 0:
        out = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    S = float(out.sum(dtype=np.float64))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 1e-12:
        out = np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
        S = 1.0
    targetS = math.sqrt(2.0 * int(out.size))
    out = out * (targetS / S)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, 1000.0)

    # If the exact evaluator is available, verify/rectify the final answer.
    rem = float(hard_deadline) - time.monotonic()
    exact_candidates = [out]
    if stored is not None and int(stored.size) <= 16384:
        s_store = _normalize_sequence(stored, math.sqrt(2.0 * int(stored.size)))
        if s_store is not None:
            exact_candidates.append(s_store)

    if rem > 3.0:
        best_exact_seq = out
        best_exact_score = _local_score(out)
        for cand in exact_candidates:
            if int(cand.size) <= 16384 and (time.monotonic() < hard_deadline - 0.5):
                es = _safe_exact_score([float(x) for x in cand])
                if np.isfinite(es) and es < best_exact_score:
                    best_exact_score = es
                    best_exact_seq = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).copy()
        if np.isfinite(best_exact_score) and best_exact_score < global_score:
            out = best_exact_seq
            global_score = best_exact_score

    try:
        print(f"final length={int(out.size)} local_score={float(global_score):.9f}")
    except Exception:
        pass

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
